00047: def cube(owner, name, size, pos, col):
00048:     return owner.include(name, box(size, center=pos, color=col))
00049: 
00050: def bar(owner, name, a, b, r, col=IRON):
00051:     return owner.include(name, rod(radius=r, start=a, end=b, color=col))
00052: 
00053: def ring(owner, name, center, radius, tube, color, segments=24, plane="xz"):
00054:     p = Part(name)
00055:     cx, cy, cz = center
00056:     pts = []
00057:     for i in range(segments):
00058:         t = 2*math.pi*i/segments
00059:         if plane == "xz":
00060:             pts.append((cx+radius*math.cos(t), cy, cz+radius*math.sin(t)))
00061:         elif plane == "yz":
00062:             pts.append((cx, cy+radius*math.cos(t), cz+radius*math.sin(t)))
00063:         else:
00064:             pts.append((cx+radius*math.cos(t), cy+radius*math.sin(t), cz))
00065:     for i in range(segments):
00066:         bar(p, "rim_%02d" % i, pts[i], pts[(i+1) % segments], tube, color)
00067:     return owner.include(name, p)
00068: 
00069: def wheel(owner, name, center, radius, color, spokes=5):
00070:     p = Part(name)
00071:     x, y, z = center
00072:     ring(p, "outer", center, radius, .025, color)
00073:     ring(p, "inner_edge", (x,y+.022,z), radius-.035, .011, WORN)
00074:     bar(p, "hub", (x,y-.065,z), (x,y+.075,z), .068, color)
00075:     for i in range(spokes):
00076:         a = 2*math.pi*i/spokes+.2
00077:         b = a+.20
00078:         start = (x+.055*math.cos(a), y, z+.055*math.sin(a))
00079:         bend = (x+radius*.57*math.cos(b), y, z+radius*.57*math.sin(b))
00080:         end = (x+(radius-.025)*math.cos(a+.12),
00081:                y, z+(radius-.025)*math.sin(a+.12))
00082:         bar(p, "spoke_%d_a" % i, start, bend, .024, color)
00083:         bar(p, "spoke_%d_b" % i, bend, end, .024, color)
00084:     bar(p, "crank_pin", (x+radius*.68,y,z-.08),
00085:         (x+radius*.68,y-.13,z-.08), .024, WORN)
00086:     return owner.include(name, p)

00314: # Appearance comparison: render 1 is broadly paler and more evenly illuminated
00315: # than the photograph, especially the bench and floor. That remains an appearance
00316: # hypothesis for a later controlled revision; lights/materials are held fixed here.
00317: #
00318: # Inline pre-execution self-review (code review, not a regression-test claim):
00319: # Inventory and ownership retained. Attached lathe nodes are returned by include,
00320: # so wheels/pedestals stay descendants of their complete semantic owners.
00321: # Actual supports: bench z=1.04, stump top z=1.04, sill top ~1.86,
00322: # bins/posts at floor ~0. No new support or dependency transform is introduced.
00323: # Expected negative space in wheel rims, between legs, and inside drawer retained.
00324: # Approximately 700 primitive meshes; wheel rims/spokes are the largest repeated
00325: # decorative family, followed by 43 floorboards and two 16-segment bin walls.
00326: # Ring builder expands to one rod per segment; no fillets or dense straight sweeps.
00327: # All dimensions/radii are positive and finite; all rod endpoints distinct.
00328: # Include slots are unique within each owner. No geometry is created after realize.
00329: # Camera/world/lights follow realize. Shader traversal below asserts nonempty
00330: # semantic descendants and copies mesh/material state before scoped edits.
00331: # Only CAMERA differs intentionally from the successful construction/appearance.
00332: 
00333: realize(scene)
00334: 
00335: def mesh_descendants(marker_name):
00336:     root = bpy.data.objects.get(marker_name)
00337:     if root is None:
00338:         candidates = [o for o in bpy.data.objects
00339:                       if o.type == "EMPTY" and o.name.split(".")[0] == marker_name]
00340:         assert candidates, marker_name
00341:         root = candidates[0]

00058: def cube(owner, name, size, pos, col):
00059:     return owner.include(name, box(size, center=pos, color=col))
00060: 
00061: def bar(owner, name, a, b, r, col=IRON):
00062:     return owner.include(name, rod(radius=r, start=a, end=b, color=col))
00063: 
00064: def ring(owner, name, center, radius, tube, color, segments=24, plane="xz"):
00065:     p = Part(name)
00066:     cx, cy, cz = center
00067:     pts = []
00068:     for i in range(segments):
00069:         t = 2 * math.pi * i / segments
00070:         if plane == "xz":
00071:             pts.append((cx + radius*math.cos(t), cy, cz + radius*math.sin(t)))
00072:         elif plane == "yz":
00073:             pts.append((cx, cy + radius*math.cos(t), cz + radius*math.sin(t)))
00074:         else:
00075:             pts.append((cx + radius*math.cos(t), cy + radius*math.sin(t), cz))
00076:     for i in range(segments):
00077:         bar(p, "rim_%02d" % i, pts[i], pts[(i+1) % segments], tube, color)
00078:     return owner.include(name, p)
00079: 
00080: def wheel(owner, name, center, radius, color, spokes=5):
00081:     p = Part(name)
00082:     x, y, z = center
00083:     ring(p, "outer", center, radius, .025, color)
00084:     ring(p, "inner_edge", (x, y+.022, z), radius-.035, .011, WORN)
00085:     bar(p, "hub", (x, y-.065, z), (x, y+.075, z), .068, color)
00086:     for i in range(spokes):
00087:         a = 2*math.pi*i/spokes + .2
00088:         b = a + .20
00089:         start = (x+.055*math.cos(a), y, z+.055*math.sin(a))
00090:         bend = (x+radius*.57*math.cos(b), y, z+radius*.57*math.sin(b))
00091:         end = (x+(radius-.025)*math.cos(a+.12), y,
00092:                z+(radius-.025)*math.sin(a+.12))
00093:         bar(p, "spoke_%d_a" % i, start, bend, .024, color)
00094:         bar(p, "spoke_%d_b" % i, bend, end, .024, color)
00095:     bar(p, "crank_pin", (x+radius*.68, y, z-.08),
00096:         (x+radius*.68, y-.13, z-.08), .024, WORN)
00097:     return owner.include(name, p)

00326: def mesh_descendants(marker_name):
00327:     root = bpy.data.objects.get(marker_name)
00328:     if root is None:
00329:         candidates = [o for o in bpy.data.objects
00330:                       if o.type == "EMPTY" and o.name.split(".")[0] == marker_name]
00331:         assert candidates, marker_name
00332:         root = candidates[0]
00333:     meshes = [o for o in root.children_recursive if o.type == "MESH"]
00334:     assert meshes, marker_name
00335:     return meshes
00336: 
00337: def add_surface_variation(marker_name, scale, strength):
00338:     for ob in mesh_descendants(marker_name):
00339:         ob.data = ob.data.copy()
00340:         for idx, mat in enumerate(ob.data.materials):
00341:             if mat is None:
00342:                 continue
00343:             mat = mat.copy()
00344:             ob.data.materials[idx] = mat
00345:             mat.use_nodes = True
00346:             nt = mat.node_tree
00347:             bs = nt.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00348:             if bs is None:
00349:                 continue
00350:             bs.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = .87
00351:             tex = nt.nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00352:             tex.inputs["Scale"].default_value = scale
00353:             tex.inputs["Detail"].default_value = 2
00354:             bump = nt.nodes.new("ShaderNodeBump")
00355:             bump.inputs["Strength"].default_value = strength
00356:             bump.inputs["Distance"].default_value = .035
00357:             nt.links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"], bump.inputs["Height"])
00358:             nt.links.new(bump.outputs["Normal"], bs.inputs["Normal"])
